國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選舉進入
最後衝刺階段 
經濟 
⚫ 美國第三季實質 GDP 季增年率(初值)由上季的 2.2%升至 3.4%，成長主
要受民間消費 、 企業投資及庫存回補帶動 。 其中個人消費貢獻2.52 個百
分點、私人投資貢獻 3.27 個百分點，惟淨出口拖累 3.32 個百分點，整
體經濟仍維持穩健擴張。 
⚫ 9 月美國新屋銷售年化 67.6 萬戶，月減 0.6%、年減 5.3%，主要是中西
部及南部地區銷售下滑拖累整體表現。房價雖較上月回升，但仍低於去
年同期；供給月數 8.4 個月，顯示市場仍呈供給充裕、需求偏弱格局。 
⚫ 美國 9 月個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數年增率由 3.4%升至 3.6%，核心
PCE 物價指數年增率亦由 3.0%升至 3.1%，通膨再度升溫。儘管實質所
得受物價上漲影響而下滑，消費支出仍持續成長，反映家庭需求及內需
動能仍具韌性。 
⚫ 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 決議將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%
至 4.0%。決策聲明指出，美國經濟活動表現強勁，就業市場變化不大，
通膨持續高於 2%目標，將依後續數據評估適當政策立場，以確保通膨
回歸目標。兩名委員主張升息 1 碼，顯示內部對通膨風險仍存分歧。主
席 Kevin Warsh 強調將防止能源價格上漲擴散至整體物價 ， 並宣布2027
年起部分 FOMC 會議改為策略討論會議，以強化長期政策研議。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月 26 日路透社民調顯示，川普施政支持率 32%，略高於前週；國會
選舉政黨支持度則由民主黨 47%領先共和黨 36%。截至 10 月 31 日，
提前及郵寄投票已逾 5,100 萬人。整體而言，期中選舉結果將影響未來
國會版圖、施政推動及貿易政策方向，備受市場關注。 
短評 
⚫ 美國第三季 GDP 成長優於前季，主要受民間消費、企業投資及庫存回
補帶動，顯示內需與企業活動仍具韌性；惟新屋銷售持續疲弱、房市需
求未明顯改善，反映高利率環境仍抑制部分經濟活動。另一方面，PCE
通膨再度升溫，核心通膨降幅有限，顯示物價壓力仍存。在經濟穩健且
通膨高於目標情況下，Fed 維持利率不變並釋出偏鷹訊號，後續貨幣政
策料將維持審慎，持續關注通膨與就業數據變化。

但隨通膨風險持續上行，後續仍具升息空間，貨幣政策可望維持漸進緊
縮基調。

國 
工業部門成本轉嫁能力有限，使利潤率持續萎縮 
經濟 ⚫ 1-9 月工業部門營業收入、利潤年增率分別為 15.5、13.1%。

腐與紀律，沒有推出重大經濟改革方案。 
短評 ⚫ 工業部門營收受到物價上漲帶動，但成本轉嫁能力有限，反而降低利潤 表現。

增速料將放緩，後續仍須關注 AI 需求延續性、成長動能向傳統產業擴

兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.11.2 
一、 美國—經濟維持穩健擴張，通膨壓力略升溫，Fed 維持利率不變，期中選
舉進入最後衝刺階段 
2026 年第三季美國實質國內生產毛額(GDP)季增年率初值為 3.4%， 高於第二
季之 2.2%，整體景氣持續穩健擴張。就成長貢獻而言，個人消費支出仍為經濟成
長主要支柱，貢獻 2.52 個百分點，其中服務消費及商品消費分別貢獻 1.89 個百
分點及 0.63 個百分點 ， 反映民間需求維持穩健 ； 私人投資則貢獻3.27 個百分點 ，
為本季成長最大亮點，其中固定投資貢獻1.66 個百分點，而企業庫存回補亦額外
貢獻 1.61 個百分點。對外貿易方面，淨出口拖累經濟成長 3.32 個百分點，其中
出口減少造成 0.68 個百分點負面影響；進口增加則拖累 2.64 個百分點。政府消
費支出與投資則受聯邦政府非國防支出增加帶動，貢獻 0.90 個百分點。此外，第
三季個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數及核心 PCE 物價指數分別年增 2.1%及 2.5%，
均低於第二季水準。整體而言，美國第三季經濟成長主要受民間消費、企業投資
及庫存回補支撐 ， 雖貿易逆差擴大對GDP 形成明顯拖累 ， 但內需表現仍具韌性 ，
顯示美國經濟基本面維持穩健。 
2026 年 9 月美國新屋銷售經季節性調整後年化戶數為 67.6 萬戶，較上月減
少 0.6%， 並較去年同期下降5.3%， 顯示在高利率環境下住宅需求仍較去年疲弱 。
地區別表現分歧，其中東北部及西部分別較上月增加 28.0%及 21.4%，惟中西部
及南部分別減少 7.3%及 6.6%，尤其占全美銷售比重最大的南部地區回落，成為
拖累整體銷售的主因。價格方面，9 月新屋銷售中位價為 40.04 萬美元，較上月
上升 3.9%，但較去年同期下跌 3.9%，儘管房價高於上月，惟仍面臨修正壓力。
供給方面，9 月底待售新屋數量為 47.6 萬戶，較上月減少1.0%，較去年同期減少
2.3%；按目前銷售速度計算，供給月數為 8.4 個月，低於上月的 8.5 個月，但高
於去年同期的 8.2 個月。整體而言，9 月美國新屋銷售較前月小幅下滑且年減幅
度擴大，反映購屋需求仍未明顯改善；雖然部分地區銷售回升且庫存略有下降，
但供給月數仍維持相對偏高水準，顯示新屋市場仍處於供給較充裕、需求偏弱的
格局，後續仍須觀察利率走勢及就業市場變化對住宅需求的影響。 
2026 年 9 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增 0.5%， 高於8 月之 0.3%，

年增率則由 3.4%升至 3.6%； 扣除食品及能源後之核心PCE 月增率維持於 0.3%，
年增率由 3.0%微升至 3.1%，能源價格回升及服務價格上漲持續對整體物價形成
支撐，通膨再度升溫。另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)亦月增
0.2%，個人消費支出則月增 0.5%，增幅高於所得成長，顯示家庭消費需求仍具韌
性；惟受物價上漲影響，實質可支配所得月減0.3%，實質個人消費支出則持平，
反映名目支出增加部分已被物價上升所抵銷。整體而言，美國 9 月消費活動維持
穩健，內需仍為經濟成長提供支撐，但通膨壓力較前月略有升溫，物價回落至聯
準會 2%目標之過程仍具挑戰，後續仍須持續關注能源價格變化、核心通膨走勢
及消費動能對聯準會貨幣政策方向之影響。 
聯準會(Fed)於 2026 年 10 月 FOMC 會議以 10 比 2 表決結果，決定將聯邦基
金利率目標區間維持於 3.75%至 4.00%不變，符合市場預期；謹將本次會議聲明
及主席 Kevin Warsh 記者會重點摘錄如下：(一)聯準會認為美國經濟活動持續以
強勁步調擴張，較前次聲明所稱之「穩健擴張」進一步上修對經濟表現之評價；
儘管地緣政治情勢使經濟前景不確定性仍偏高，但國內支出維持韌性，生產力成
長與資本投資表現強勁，就業增幅大致與勞動力成長同步，失業率變動不大，顯
示整體經濟基本面仍具相當韌性；(二)通膨仍高於聯準會 2%目標，委員會表示需
累積更多經濟與物價資訊，以評估適當政策立場，確保通膨適時回歸 2%目標，
並重申將致力維持物價穩定 ；(三)本次決議出現兩張反對票 ， 聯準會理事Lisa Cook
及克里夫蘭聯邦準備銀行總裁 Beth Hammack 主張升息 1 碼，反映部分委員對通
膨風險仍抱持較為謹慎態度；(四)主席 Kevin Warsh 於記者會表示，近期聯邦公
開市場委員會討論焦點多集中於物價穩定，特別是在近期能源及相關價格再度上
揚情況下，聯準會有責任避免個別產業價格上漲擴散至整體經濟，並確保通膨預
期維持穩定；(五)Warsh 並宣布調整 FOMC 會議安排，2027 年仍將維持每年 8 次
會議，但其中 1 次將改為專門討論貨幣政策策略之會議，不進行利率表決或發布
政策聲明，並預期 2028 年將有 2 次會議採取此模式，以騰出更多時間進行長期
政策與重大議題研議。整體而言，本次會議雖維持利率不變，但聯準會仍認為通
膨問題尚未完全解決，後續政策方向仍將取決於通膨、勞動市場及整體經濟數據
表現。 
根據 10 月 26 日路透社最新民調，總統川普施政支持率為 32%，較前一週之
31%略為回升；在國會選舉政黨支持度方面， 47%的登記選民表示傾向支持民主

支持度上仍具優勢，但相關結果僅反映調查期間之民意分布，尚難直接推論最終

州的選務程序、郵寄選票資格認定及選舉公正性相關爭議亦持續受到輿論關注。
整體而言，本次期中選舉結果除將影響國會兩院席次分布外，亦可能牽動川普政
府後續施政推動、預算協商及貿易政策方向，受到國際市場高度關注。 
二、歐元區—歐洲央行如期暫停升息，12 月升息機率較高 
 歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率為 0.4%，但有部份是受到愛爾蘭經濟成長率大幅
波動的扭曲，若剔除愛爾蘭，則歐元區第三季 GDP 季增率僅約 0.2%。該季受到
物價壓力上升 、 地緣政治緊張局勢等因素影響 ， 對消費及投資需求構成負面因素 ，
但世足賽以及德國財政支出擴張的支持下，仍使歐元區經濟保持正向表現。就國
別而言，德、法、義、西GDP 季增率分別為 1.2%、-0.1%、0.8%、2.7%；其中德
國經濟活動受到財政擴張的提振；法國的消費支出雖強勁，但受到投資減緩和進
口增加拖累；義大利製造業產出萎縮拖累成長表現；西班牙在住宅營建熱潮的支
持下， 經濟活動持續優於其他國家， 但歐盟NGEU 計畫到期以及政府財政整頓力
道，西班牙後續經濟動能可能會逐漸放緩。 
 歐洲央行在 10 月 29 日宣布維持政策利率不變，主要是近期歐元區各國殖利
率普遍上升 ， 對經濟造成限制性因素 ， 且不確定性可能會隨著時間的推移而累積 ，
從而拖累經濟成長。不過，歐洲央行仍認為近期高漲的能源價格對通膨的壓力相
當大，使央行考慮進一步採取貨幣政策措施因應。因此，除非經濟成長受到超出
預期的衝擊、能源價格下跌或金融環境急劇收緊，一般預期歐洲央行在 12 月的
政策會議將會宣布升息一碼。 
三、日本—日本央行暫停升息，通膨壓力續存，升息預期升溫 
 9 月工業生產月增 2.2%，扭轉 8 月受天災造成生產中斷而下滑的情勢，從細

月成長，反映 AI 相關資本支出需求持續暢旺；庫存月增 2.5%，石化產品庫存亦
逐步回補。展望未來，製造商預估 10 月及 11 月產量將分別月增 5.1%及 0.8%，

(1) 新加坡：9 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增率小幅回落至 2.2%、核心通膨率自 8

(2) 泰國：9 月出口額達 384.4 億美元、年增 24.0%，創歷史新高，主要得益於工
業產品 出口成長 25.4%，其中， 以電腦及相關設備（ 59.4%）、電話及零件
（117.5%）及電路板（45.9%）成長最為顯著，對美國出口額年增 50.1%，對
中國和日本的出口額分別成長 18.4%和 19.0%。9 月進口額 443 億美元、年增
50.4%，創歷史新高，因出口導向製造業生產需求及全球能源價格的上漲，主
要類別進口額均有所成長，其中，資本貨物(電腦、機械零件、電路板等)進口
大增 92.3%，燃料、原材料和中間產品進口增幅均逾 4 成、運輸設備在飛機與
航空設備進口大增 12 倍下成長 24%。9 月貿易逆差大幅擴大至 59 億美元，累
計 1 至 9 月 ， 貿易逆差達 437 億美元 ， 出口額成長19.6%、進口額成長 37.8%。

支持經濟復甦的不平衡 。經濟預估方面 ，強於預期的出口以及與科技和人工智
慧週期相關的投資，預估 2026 年和 2027 年經濟成長率將分別成長 2.4%和
2.1%，然而，高額的家庭債務、脆弱的勞動市場和疲軟的購買力繼續抑制私人
消費，使經濟成長仍低於潛在水平。通膨方面，受能源價格上漲和聖嬰現象的
影響，預計通膨率在 2027 年初將出現短暫上升，之後隨著國內需求的疲軟而
回落，2026 年和 2027 年整體通膨率平均分別為 2.1%和 1.3%。

六、台灣—AI 動能續強，經濟成長動能穩健 
9 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，較上月增加 1 分，燈號
續呈紅燈，其中工業及服務業加班工時，受惠 AI 相關產業生產動能維持熱絡，
由綠燈轉呈黃紅燈，其餘八項燈號均維持不變。另領先與同時指標皆呈上升，顯
示國內經濟動能維持穩健。 
主計總處 10 月 30 日公布台灣第三季經濟成長率為 14.36%，主要受 AI 需求
強勁帶動出口及企業投資大幅成長，民間消費亦維持熱絡，顯示外需動能正逐步
向內需擴散，整體經濟呈現內外皆熱。展望未來，AI 需求、半導體相關投資及企
業資本支出持續擴張，加上年底消費旺季，可望支撐短期景氣表現；惟今年經濟
高速成長墊高比較基期，明年增速料將放緩，後續仍須關注 AI 需求延續性、成
長動能向傳統產業擴散情形及金融市場波動等不確定因素。

## Document 073: 2026年7-9月期GDP予測（1次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年7-9月期GDP予測（1次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026 年 10 月30 日

1/3 
2026 年 7-9 月期 GDP 予測（1 次速報） 
～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～ 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

小幅ながら４四半期連続のプラス成長を予想、景気回復基調は維持 
11 月16 日に公表される2026 年７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率を前期比年率＋0.4％（前期比＋
0.1％）と予測する。４四半期連続のプラス成長になるだろう1。 
物価高による下押しや耐久財需要の反動に加え、天候不順の影響もあって個人消費が減少する
一方、設備投資が高い伸びになることが下支えになる見込みである。設備投資の伸びには特殊要
因も一部含まれているが、それを除いても堅調だったと考えられる。個人消費が弱含むなかでも
プラス成長を維持するとみられることは、企業部門の底堅さが景気を下支えしていることを示し
ている。過去３四半期に比較的高い成長が続いた後であることも踏まえれば、今回の減速をもっ
て景気の基調が弱まったと判断する必要はないだろう。家計部門の弱さは懸念材料だが、企業部
門の底堅さは維持されており、景気は引き続き緩やかな回復基調にあると評価できる。

輸入増による成長率押し下げと公的在庫のプラス寄与は表裏一体 
７-９月期のＧＤＰを評価する際には、原油輸入と公的在庫の動きをセットで捉える必要があ
る。４-６月期は、中東情勢の悪化によって原油輸入が激減したことが、ＧＤＰ統計上は輸入減を
通じて成長率を押し上げた。一方、輸入の減少による国内供給の不足を補うために国家備蓄原油
が放出され、公的在庫の取り崩しが進んだ。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.5％Pt
（前期比年率寄与度▲2.1％Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し下げた。輸入減による成長率の押し上げ
は、公的在庫の減少と表裏一体の関係にあった。 
７-９月期は、これと逆の動きが生じるとみられる。政府・民間による代替調達の進展によって
原油輸入が回復するため、統計上は輸入増が成長率の押し下げ要因になる。一方、輸入の回復に
伴って国家備蓄原油の取り崩しは一服しており、公的在庫の減少幅は前期に比べて大きく縮小す
るとみられる。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度＋0.4％Pt（前期比年率寄与度＋1.5％
Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し上げることが予想される。

1 10 月 30 日時点で入手可能な経済指標を元に作成している。国際収支や家計調査等、今後公表される経済指標の結果
を踏まえて予測値を修正する可能性がある。

### Page 2

2/3 
輸入増による成長率の押し下げと公的在庫変動による押し上げは、ともに原油の供給環境が改
善したことに伴う動きである。両者を個別に景気の強弱として評価することは適切ではなく、一
体として見るべきだろう。 
むしろ重要なのは、代替調達の進展によって国家備蓄への依存を減らしながら必要な原油を確
保できる状況になり、資源不足が生産活動を大きく制約するリスクが低下していることである。
ＧＤＰ統計上、輸入の増加は成長率の押し下げ要因となるものの、経済実態としては国内で利用
可能な資源の供給回復を意味している。この点は、７-９月期の日本経済を評価するうえで、むし
ろ前向きに捉えるべきだろう。

需要項目別の動向 
個人消費は前期比▲0.2％と小幅減少を予想する。８～９月に各地で相次いだ大雨や台風などの
天候不順が外出機会やサービス消費を抑制した可能性がある。また、４-６月期には自動車やエア
コンなどの耐久財販売が増加したが、７-９月期にはこうした一時的な押し上げ効果が剥落した。
加えて、物価高による節約志向が根強いなか、食料品や日用品などの非耐久財消費にも弱さがみ
られる。高い賃上げや底堅い雇用環境は下支え要因となっているものの、天候不順という一時的
な要因もあって個人消費は総じて力強さを欠く結果になったとみられる。 
設備投資は前期比＋2.0％と、高い伸びを予想する。４-６月期には特許権の海外譲渡に伴う研
究開発投資の減少という特殊要因があったが、７-９月期はその影響が剥落することが押し上げ要
因になった面はある。もっとも、こうした特殊要因を除いても設備投資は明確に増加したと考え
られ、底堅い動きとなっている。企業収益が高水準を維持するなか、人手不足に対応した省力化
投資やデジタル・ＡＩ関連投資への需要は根強く、企業の設備投資意欲は引き続き強い。設備投
資計画も高い伸びとなっており、設備投資は均してみれば緩やかな増加基調にあると判断してよ
いだろう。 
なお、特許権の海外への譲渡により、テクニカルに４-６月期はサービス輸出が上振れ、設備投
資が下振れたが、７-９月期はその剥落によりサービス輸出が下振れ、設備投資が上振れとなる見
込みである。両者は相殺し合うため、成長率全体でみれば概ねニュートラルとなる。 
輸出は前期比＋1.2％と４四半期連続の増加を予想する。４-６月期に大口要因によって研究開
発サービスの受取が急増した分が剥落することで、サービス輸出が減少する一方、財輸出が高い
伸びとなり、財貨・サービス輸出全体では増加を維持したとみられる。半導体製造装置などの資
本財輸出が好調だった。地域別では、ＥＵ向けが低調だった一方、ＡＳＥＡＮ、ＮＩＥｓ向けの
資本財輸出が伸びている。また、４-６月期に急減した中東向け輸出が持ち直したことも押し上げ
につながっている。 
輸入は前期比＋3.3％と大幅な増加が予想される。４-６月期には資源輸入の激減により輸入が
大幅に減少していたが、代替調達の進展により７-９月期は明確に持ち直した。この結果、外需寄
与度は前期比▲0.3％Pt（前期比年率▲1.3％Pt）になったと予想される。 
ただし、この外需寄与度のマイナスは、資源輸入の回復に伴う輸入の増加によって生じるもの

### Page 3

なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
である。その裏側では公的在庫変動が前期比＋0.4％ポイント、前期比年率では＋1.5％ポイント
の大幅プラス寄与になると見込まれる。両者は併せて評価する必要がある。 
民間在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.1％Ptと予想する。製品在庫はプラスが予想される一方、流通
在庫の下押しが大きくなるとみられる。 
住宅投資は前期比▲0.9％と、２四半期連続の減少を予想する。建設コストの上昇が下押し要因
となっており、住宅投資を取り巻く環境は引き続き厳しい。また、公共投資も同▲1.5％と減少を
予想するほか、政府消費についても、４-６月期の高い伸びの反動から前期比▲0.3％と減少を見
込む。

## Document 123: 261029_daiwa_RTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/261029_daiwa_RTK.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: On 29 October, Realtek reported 3Q26 results that missed our estimates but 
were in line with the consensus. This was mainly due to weaker-than-expected revenue 
likely on a deeper demand rebalancing despite pricing power on supply-chain cost 
passthrough. While 4Q26 outlook looks intact, management expects steady topline growth 
into 2027, supported by a multi-year bandwidth upgrade cycle, continued pricing resilience 
and emerging AI opportunities from cloud to edge to physical AI, despite likely still subdued 
PC and consumer electronics demand. We rate the stock Outperform (2) premised upon 
our key thesis of a multi-year/multi-G (gigabits per second) bandwidth upgrade cycle 
across wireline and wireless to benefit Realtek, which is further echoed by management’s 
2027 outlook.

Highlight 
• 3Q26 results miss on lower topline. Realtek reported 3Q26 net profit of TWD4.1bn, 
missing our estimate by 7% while in line with the consensus. With financial metrics such 
as margins broadly in line, the miss was mainly due to lower-than-expected topline 
which came in 8% below our estimate at TWD37bn. We believe the weak revenue was 
driven by a deeper demand rebalancing after customer restocking and pre-stocking in 
1H26 on seasonality and supply-chain uncertainties, flagged in our note, Downgrading: 
pricing to bridge rebalancing but …, 4 August. Although Realtek raised prices to pass on 
cost inflation to the supply chain, it failed to offset such rebalancing. As usual, 
management gave no quantitative guidance for 4Q26 but expressing “prudent and 
steady” revenue run rates, in line with our expectations. We will be reviewing our model 
in due course.

• Key takeaways. At today’s results call, management further expected a steady topline 
growth heading into 2027, driven by: 1) multiple-G bandwidth upgrade cycles across 
wireline and wireless networking, including Wi-Fi 7 and 8, multi-G Ethernet, PON and 
switch, to enhance product mix with market-share gains adding to upside; 2) likely 
sustained pricing power with the company continuing to pass on inflated costs to the 
supply chain; and 3) despite likely still subdued demand from PC and consumer 
electronics markets, the ongoing development of AI, from cloud to edge AI to physical AI 
applications, is creating growth opportunities and tallying with our investment thesis of 
the multi-year/multi-G bandwidth upgrade cycle to structurally benefit Realtek.

Recommendation 
We rate Realtek Outperform (2). The stock price has generally consolidated around 
TWD670-790 over the past 3 months due to lack of catalysts amid its demand rebalancing, 
suggesting patience for investors. Key downside risk to our call would be any commodity 
price hikes leading to worse-than-expected demand rebalancing.

Idea
Further upside surprise: In our recent CPO report, we raised WIN Semi's revenue
forecasts  to reflect stronger demand for satellite communications RF, driven by
major customer SpaceX (SPCX; covered by Adam Jonas). However, we
underestimated the favorable pricing and margin, as evidenced by the 28 Oct   3Q26
results. Reflecting the much higher gross margin, we raise our 2026, 2027 and 2028
EPS by 35%, 28% and 17%, respectively, and raise our price target 17% to  NT$700  to

capture the long-term upside from both new businesses - space communications
and AI optical networking laser.
3Q26 margin beat: Core margin reached 40.7% in 3Q26, well above our original
forecast of 29%. The company indicated space communications revenue already
represents 40% of its infrastructure business, accounting for 35-40% of total
revenue. This implies the space-related revenue is tracking at c.15% of total revenue
in 3Q. Despite a relatively  small portion of revenue, we estimate the business could

lift 3Q26 margin by 5% (half of 3Q26 increase), implying the space communications
margin could be >60%, well above our and Street expectations. V3 satellite
development will be a key growth driver into 2027. See Exhibit 1  for our analysis.
4Q26 revenue guidance looks solid: Smartphone and WiFi revenue remain stable
despite weak China smartphone demand: Win Semi now has higher exposure to
Apple iPhone through high-band PA (Broadcom) and WiFi (Murata). As such,

revenue can still grow single digit QoQ in 4Q26, despite recent weakness in China
smartphone demand. Please see Exhibit 3  and Exhibit 4    for  our earnings review.
Upgrade to OW: The stock trades at 25x our new 2028 EPS. Given the significant
revenue contribution from space communications and AI lasers, we think our
implied target multiple of 29x 2028 EPS should be justified. Win Semi historically
traded at 40x PE during the smartphone 3D sensing product cycle.

2
Surprising Margin Expansion from Optical/Space Businesses
WIN Semi   could benefit from PD growth and potential LD outsourcing
by late 2027
In our previous WIN Semi report, we argued that Lumentum (LITE.O, covered by Meta
Marshall) was unlikely to outsource CW lasers or EML production to WIN Semi, a key
pillar of our earlier UW rating. However, our recent  checks with WIN Semi management
suggest that WIN Semi and its major customer, we assume it's Broadcom (AVGO, covered

by Joseph Moore), have secured sufficient InP substrate supply for WIN Semi's PD
production, potentially supporting substantial growth for the PD (photo diode) business
in 2H26. We estimate PD could account for >10% of WIN Semi's total revenue. Our recent
checks from the supply chain also suggest that WIN Semi's largest PD customer  could
outsource LD (laser diode) production to WIN Semi.
We also see the potential for even stronger revenue growth from WIN Semi's aerospace

business into 2027, especially from the ground station and satellite business. With
SpaceX's  launch of Starship and Starlink V3 satellite, we expect WIN Semi's content value
per satellite   to increase significantly from Starlink v2 to v3, with potential upside from
future DTD (direct to device) satellites. The increase in content value comes not only from
higher RF chips volumes, but also from the migration from GaAs to GaN. Based on our
industry checks, the ASP for 4-inch GaN could be US$9k, compared with ~US$2.5-3k for 6-

inch GaAs. We therefore upgrade our rating from EW to OW with the strong gross margin
performance, which we believe to be >60%.
Where we could be wrong with our OW call: We think the largest near-term swing factor
for WIN Semi is the aerospace business, which is relatively difficult to track given the
inherent risk of delays or failures in satellite launches. However, on September 28, 2026,
SpaceX successfully launched the Starship with Starlink V3 satellite. If SpaceX decides to

speed up the satellite constellation, there also could be upside to our forecasts.
All of our satellite launch numbers are based on Adam Jonas's SpaceX forecasts. Please
see his latest report: SpaceX: SpaceX/Tesla and SI's Next Act (23 Oct 2026)

3Q26 earnings and 4Q26 guidance review - margin and earnings beat on

strong optical and aerospace demand
WIN Semi delivered a solid 3Q26 performance, with margins and earnings exceeding
expectations, supported by robust demand across its optical and aerospace businesses.
The gross margin beat MSe and consensus by 7-8ppts.  Strength in optical applications,
driven by continued investment in AI-related optical connectivity and high-speed data
transmission, alongside resilient aerospace demand, supported a favorable product mix

and improved manufacturing efficiency.
The higher contribution from these relatively high-value-added segments lifted gross
margins above expectations, driving positive operating leverage and stronger-than-
anticipated earnings. Looking ahead, we expect sustained momentum in optical
communications and aerospace applications to remain key drivers of WIN Semi's
profitability.

WIN Semi: Valuation Methodology
Our price target (base-case value) increases from NT$600 to NT$700: This also factors
in our EPS forecast changes  for 2025 to 2028.
We continue to derive our price target from a residual income model as we believe it best
presents the value of the company. All of our other key assumptions are unchanged,
including a cost of equity of 8.0% (beta of 1.0, equity risk premium of 6% and risk-free

rate of 2%), intermediate growth rate assumption of 15% and terminal growth rate of
4.0%.
Our bull-case value  increases  from NT$900 to NT$1050 and our bear-case value from NT
$300 to NT$350, implying 44x and 14x 2028e EPS, respectively.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 153: 261102_gs_delta

Source PDF: all report pdf/261102_gs_delta.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

Key takeaways from Delta’s results call (October 29th) include: 
(1) Management expects both revenue and GM to go up sequentially 
in 4Q26, with some price adjustment likely to occur in 4Q26/1Q27. 
4Q GM could further improve due to some reversal in inventory 
write-offs, as the weaker-than-expected 3Q26 GM was primarily 
due to the large inventory write-offs with ~2.0ppt negative impact 
(we observed a sharp expansion in GM by 3.2ppt in 2024 following 
significant inventory write-offs of ~2.0ppt in 2023). Looking ahead, 
we continue to see increasing AI exposure for Delta to support the 
GM expansion (2026E GM at 36.1% per GSe vs. 34.3% in 2025). 
(2) The company’s BOD has approved building a new large-scale 
production site in Penang, Malaysia, and expect the site to reach 
similar scale as the Thailand operations within 5Y, subject to market 
demand (Thailand accounts for 30%+ of Delta’s total capacity per 
our estimate). 
(3) For HVDC products, the company started to see some initial 
shipment for +-400VDC solution, which is in line with expectation 
and mentioned that 800VDC solution is driven by Nvidia that is 
progressing more slowly (we expect 800VDC contribution to 
increase sharply along with the ramp up of next-generation AI 
server in 2H27). The company is positive that datacenters 
architecture will migrate increasingly toward HVDC in the longer 
term, but did mention some near term risk in the pace of 
deployment due to the shortage of skilled labor. 
(4) In terms of new products, Delta mentioned SOFC product will 
enter MP in 2027E, with the first production line having capacity of 
100MW+ (equivalent to US$300-400mn revenue contribution if fully 
utilized per GSe), which is mainly for sampling with clients. Several 
large clients have expressed interest in testing the new product, 
further capacity expansion will depend on successful verification, 
with equipment procurement requiring 6-10M (the company could

### Page 3

expand its SOFC capacity by multiple times within 1Y per our estimates).
(5) Delta mentioned that order visibility has extended further and noted that datacenter
deployment plans are now extended into 2030E. However, the company’s near-term
revenue and GM performance remains sensitive to the pace of client order pull-ins.
Datacenter deployment could face delays in the near term due to geopolitical risk and
shortage of components/labor/electricity, potentially leading to greater volatility in the

company’s financial performance.
(6) The company mentioned that AI already accounted for 50%+ of total revenue
(tracking faster than previous target of 25%+) and expects liquid cooling related
products to account for 13% of total revenue in 2026 (higher than previous target of
12%+).
Investment view
We maintain our positive view on Delta’s revenue/profitability outlook over the coming
years, underpinned by its increasing exposure in AI (see here). We now expect GM/OPM

will expand by 8ppt+/14ppt+ in 2028 vs. 2025, due to the increasing contribution from
the high margin AI power products (expand from 9% in 2025 to 69% in 2028E). We
believe the GM contraction in 3Q26 is manageable, with potential reversals of inventory
write-offs providing additional upside to margins going forward (historically, the
company saw solid GM expansion in 2020/2024 following significant inventory
write-offs in 2019/2023). Meanwhile, with contributions from HVDC power racks to

ramp up from 3Q26 and order visibility extending further, we expect 3Q26 GM of 34.2%
to mark the low point in 2026-28E, and we expect sequential GM expansion in every
subsequent quarter through 2028.
We believe Delta is well-positioned to capture a leading market share in the HVDC
power rack market (50-70% market share in 2027-2030E per our estimates) supported
by its industry leading AC to DC conversion capabilities. With its latest expansion in

Malaysia that is expected to reach similar scale as Thailand operations in 5Y time frame
(Thailand is currently the largest manufacturing hub for Delta), we believe Delta could
maintain its leading market share in the HVDC power rack market with its time to market
capacity. We expect HVDC solution to be the key revenue/profitability driver going
forward as its dollar content per watt to be 3x+ more than the company’s current key
PSU products.
The company continues to make progress with its new SOFC products and is expected to

ramp up in 2027 with initial capacity of 100MW+. We expect each additional 100MW of
fully utilized SOFC capacity could contribute ~1% to Delta’s 2027E revenue. We believe
Delta could capture some market share amid the electricity shortage environment, as
SOFC offers an attractive alternative power solution given its speed-to-power, reliability,
and deployment flexibility (see here).
Overall, we maintain our positive view on Delta’s long term growth prospects, supported

by its leading position in the most critical segment of the AI datacenter power industry.
We expect the company to sustain solid earnings growth over the coming year. Maintain
our Buy rating on Delta and 12m TP at NT$4,120.

Valuation 
We reiterate our Buy rating on Delta with an unchanged 12-m TP of NT$4,120, based on 
an unchanged 26.5x P/E multiple (+1.6x STDV higher than Delta’s past 15-year average 
valuation), and maintain our valuation period at 2028E and discounted back to 2027 at a 
CoE of 11%.

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Delta Electronics is the largest vendor of power supply components globally (in terms of 
sales) and a key servo motor/inverter/PLC supplier in mainland China. It is also a key 
supplier for the EV/server power industry, holding one of the largest market shares in 
both markets. We are positive on Delta’s long-term growth opportunity driven by 
multiple factors (EV/EV charger/5G infrastructure/Datacenter power/ESS etc.) in the 
coming years. We also expect demand for its AI server power and cooling systems to 
deliver solid growth given the increasing demand for high computing performance AI 
servers, which will require higher efficiency power components and better cooling 
systems to improve the overall performance. Trading at a 1-year forward P/E multiple 
that is below our target multiple, we believe the stock is undervalued, given the 
company’s strong revenue outlook (50%+ 2025-28E CAGR) for its growth sectors 
(including EV & EV charging / green capex & renewable energy / cloud computing / 
smart manufacturing / satellite communication & smart connectivity), and its leadership

potential design change on AI server DC-DC system, and (3) slower than expected of
future products deployment, including power rack, SST and SOFC.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Advantech  | Asia Pacific
October  Sales +5% MoM/+88%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Advantech's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• October sales were NT$11,336mn (+5% MoM/+88% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 35% of MSe, at NT$32,600mn (flat QoQ/+82% YoY),
and 37% of consensus, at NT$30,364mn (-6% QoQ/+69% YoY).
• By region, North America (+118%) and Korea (+112%) outperformed; other
markets, including Europe (+56%), China (+95%), Taiwan (+70%), Japan

(+44%) and emerging markets (+22%) also performed strongly.
• By segment, Intelligent Systems was +82% YoY,   IoT Automation was +24%
YoY,  Intelligent Service was +27% YoY. The Embedded segment grew 91%
YoY, within which Embedded IoT grew 124% YoY, while Applied Computing
grew 53% YoY for the month.
Our view:
• We think its 4Q26 revenue is tracking to be up QoQ, consistent with
management comments during the latest earnings call.
• Backed by the solid B/B ratio of 1.60 in October (vs. 1.64 in 3Q26), we expect

the revenue momentum to sustain into the coming quarters.
• We remain constructive on Advantech. It is well positioned to benefit from
accelerating IoT market growth amid edge AI proliferation.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

주가수익률 (%) 1개월 3개월 12개월  
 절대 (6.6) (10.0) (6.5)  
 상대 8.0  (14.2) (35.7)  
 절대 (달러환산) (5.2) (2.7) 0.2

3Q26 영업이익 1.8조, 핵심 하락 원인은 인센티브 
환율효과는 전분기 대비 -5,330억 작용했으나, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비의 급감 
(6,200억, -9,250억 qoq)으로 상쇄되었다 판단. 인센티브 비용 증가세는 -6,060억원의 영
향을 미쳤으며, 대부분 유럽지역 EV2 판매를 위한 딜러 인센티브에서 발생 추정. 
외생변수는 능력 밖의 일, EV 전략에 대해서는 장기성장에 무게 
중동 분쟁 지속, 환율 하락과 같은 외생변수의 부정적 영향을 제외한다면 단기 영업적 측면
의 실적 악재는 BEV 인센티브 지급기조의 변화가 없을 것이라는 점. 중장기 글로벌 BEV 
시장의 입지 강화를 단기 전사 수익성보다 중시하는 전략을 채택. 
마찬가지로, 4분기가 환율 효과의 본격화 
3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (QTD 1,338원) 효과로 환관련 
감익 본격화 예상. 즉, 판매보증비의 정상화 (qoq 반등)로 인해 수익성 추가 악화 가능성 존
재. 완성차 판매량의 경우 경쟁사 대비 견조했던 2Q-3Q26의 기조를 유지할 것이라 예상. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 129,000원으로 하향 
목표주가 하향 근거는 산정 기준을 FY27F로 변경함과 동시에 FY27F 자동차사업 NOPAT 
예상치 하향과 현대모비스 지분가치 하락. 밸류에이션상 저점에 도달했다는 의견은 FY27F 
EPS의 yoy 하락으로 설득력 약화. 중장기 BEV 시장 입지 강화에 대한 밸류에이션 프리미
엄 반영은 글로벌 전동화 속도에 달려 있음. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) 외생변수의 개선, 2) 신
차 출시를 통한 상품성 강화, 3) BEV 시장 점유율 확대 이후 점진적 인센티브 축소, 4) 20조
원 수준의 순현금 기반 발생 가능한 사업적 결정.

## Document 266: [005380] 현대차(005380) 어려운 업황 속 돋보이는 방어력현대차(005380) 어려운 업황 속 돋보이는 방어력어려운 업황 속 돋보이는 방어력 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 어려운 업황 속 돋보이는 방어력현대차(005380) 어려운 업황 속 돋보이는 방어력어려운 업황 속 돋보이는 방어력 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기, 물량 감소에도 확인된 이익 방어력 
현대차의 3Q26 매출액은 45조 1,100억원(Y oY -3.4%), 영업이익은 2조 6,770억원
(Y oY +5.5%), OPM 5.9%(Y oY +0.5%p)를 기록했다. 국내 파업 및 영업일수 감소 영
향으로 글로벌 도매 판매량은 92.1만대(Y oY -11.3%)까지 감소했으나, HEV 중심의 
고부가 차종 Mix 개선, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비(Y oY -1.2조원) 감소 효과
로 수익성은 오히려 개선됐다. 특히 HEV 판매 비중은 18.2%(Y oY +2.7%p)까지 상
승했다. 반면 EV 판매는 전년 대비 감소세가 이어지며 지역별 수요 둔화와 경쟁 심
화 영향이 지속됐다. 
연간 눈높이는 낮아졌지만, 믹스 개선은 지속 
현대차는 글로벌 수요 둔화와 생산 차질을 반영해 2026년 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 
기존 415.8만대에서 400만대로, 영업이익률 가이던스를 기존 6.3~7.3%에서 
5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정했다. 다만 북미를 중심으로 HEV 판매가 견조하게 유지되고 
있으며, 하반기 주요 신차 투입과 전동화 라인업 확대를 통해 믹스 개선 기조는 이
어질 전망이다. 중장기적으로는 로보택시 공급 확대와 Atlas 상용화 등 Physical AI 
사업도 계획대로 진행 중으로, 단기 판매 성장 둔화에도 자동차 본업의 수익성 방어
와 신사업 모멘텀은 유효한 것으로 판단한다. 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 460,000원 하향 
글로벌 완성차 업체들의 수익성 부담은 확대되고 있다. 3분기 자동차 부문 영업이익
률은 Mercedes-Benz 2.5%, Stellantis 0.7%, Volvo -5.6%, VW -7.8%를 기록했다. 
유럽 내 BEV 판매 확대에도 중국 전기차 업체들의 공격적인 해외 진출과 가격 경
쟁, 중국 시장 부진이 동시에 나타나면서 전통 OEM의 수익성 압박이 심화되고 있
다. 이러한 환경에서 현대차는 HEV 중심의 유연한 파워트레인 전략과 북미 중심의 
Mix 개선을 통해 5% 후반의 수익성을 유지하고 있다는 점이 상대적으로 부각된다.

## Document 291: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요삼성바이오로직스(207940) 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시_dcb09b7c.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 22

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

## Document 315: AI基建融资新裂痕！一批数据中心贷款被打包甩卖，星际之门项目也在其中

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI基建融资新裂痕！一批数据中心贷款被打包甩卖，星际之门项目也在其中.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-07
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

荷兰国际集团 ING 正寻求⼀次性抛售约 5 亿美元数据中⼼贷款组合，其中包括与 OpenAI 、
软银、甲⻣⽂联合推进的 " 星际之⻔ "AI 旗舰项⽬相关融资。更令市场警觉的是，甲⻣⽂已向
该项⽬新墨西哥州开发商发出不可抗⼒通知，暗⽰核⼼参与⽅正为退出预留法律空间。叠
加美国⼤选后科技股重挫、标普 500 蒸发逾 3 万亿美元的双重压⼒，此次抛售或成本轮 AI 基
建融资泡沫的最新裂缝。
摘要：
荷兰国际集团 ING 正寻求⼀次性出售约 5 亿美元的数据中⼼贷款组合，其中包括与 OpenAI 、软银
及甲⻣⽂联合推进的 " 星际之⻔ " （ Stargate ） AI 基础设施项⽬相关的融资，此举令本已承压的 AI
基建融资市场再添变数。
据彭博周六报道， ING 通过竞价出售流程（ bids-wanted-in-competition ）挂牌约 12 笔贷款，出
价截⽌⽇期为 11 ⽉ 19 ⽇，单笔出价⻔槛不低于 1000 万美元。
此次抛售发⽣在美国中期选举⺠主党⼤获全胜之后 —— 市场普遍担忧新政治格局将带来更严格

的科技监管，芯⽚股及新兴云计算公司遭到重创，标普 500 成分股市值合计蒸发逾 3 万亿美元。
此次出售的贷款组合中，⼀笔 2500 万美元的融资尤为引⼈关注 —— 该笔贷款与新墨西哥州⼀处
⼤型数据中⼼挂钩，该项⽬正是星际之⻔计划的重要组成部分。甲⻣⽂已就该新墨西哥州项⽬
向开发商发出不可抗⼒通知，意味着甲⻣⽂正在为可能退出相关义务预留法律空间。
星际之⻔项⽬现裂痕，不可抗⼒通知引发警觉
此次贷款出售中最受关注的细节，是甲⻣⽂向星际之⻔新墨西哥州项⽬开发商发出的不可抗⼒
通知。不可抗⼒条款通常是合同⼀⽅为摆脱既有承诺⽽启动的法律程序，意味着该⽅正在主
张 " 不可控事件 " 可免除其合同义务。
星际之⻔是美国规模最⼤的 AI 基础设施旗舰项⽬之⼀，由 OpenAI 、软银及甲⻣⽂联合推进。甲
⻣⽂在该项⽬中发出此类通知，意味着核⼼参与⽅正在重新评估项⽬推进的可⾏性，这对整个
AI 基建融资⽣态的信⼼构成直接冲击。
组合涵盖多家数据中⼼运营商，市场深度受考验
据彭博报道， ING 此次挂牌的贷款组合除星际之⻔相关融资外，还涵盖 Vantage Data Centers

及 CyrusOne 等数据中⼼运营商的债务。
数据中⼼融资在 AI 热潮期间⼀度是信贷市场最炙⼿可热的资产类别之⼀，相关贷款被打包出售给
养⽼基⾦等追求稳定收益的机构投资者。此次 5 亿美元的⼤规模集中抛售，且卖家选择匿名操
作，将直接检验在政治环境⽣变、股市⼤幅回调的双重压⼒下，这⼀市场的真实承接能⼒。
AI 基建股全线承压，融资成本上⾏⻛险浮现
此次贷款出售对权益市场的传导效应已有所显现。甲⻣⽂作为被直接点名的当事⽅，与英伟
达、微软、 CoreWeave ，以及数据中⼼地产运营商 Digital Realty 和 Equinix ⼀同承压。数据中⼼
运营商 Applied Digital 、 Cipher Digital 及 IREN 本周跌幅均超过 14% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

TUẦN 05-09/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tu ần qua, -2.62 điểm t ương ứng -0.15%, v ới thanh 
khoản cải thiện

[Vĩ mô/ Chiến lược] 
Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Nhịp điều chỉnh có th ể tiếp diễn trong các phiên t ới, song xu hư ớng tăng ng ắn 
hạn vẫn được bảo toàn, tạo cơ hội hồi phục khi VN-Index kiểm định vùng hỗ trợ 
quanh 1.710 điểm

### Page 2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -2.62 điểm tương ứng -0.15%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Công nghệ và Máy móc là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -13,987 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã HDB, PNJ và VJC; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-30 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,267 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -2,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 3,726 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -2,702 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua một tuần đi ngang giằng co, giảm nhẹ và đóng cửa ở 1.735 điểm 
với thanh khoản ở mức thấp, và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bán ròng cả 5 phiên trong tuần. 
Thông tin vĩ mô tích cực với tăng trưởng GDP quý 3 đạt mức gần 10% là điểm sáng trong 
tuần qua, tuy đã không đủ nâng đỡ thị trường chung. Thay vào đó, tâm lý thị trường thận 
trọng trước số liệu lạm phát ở mức cao, đợt tăng giá xăng dầu trong nước và mặt bằng lãi 
suất trong nước chưa có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt. Diễn biến tăng của giá dầu thế giới, và lợi suất 
trái phiếu Chính phủ Mỹ cũng là các yếu tố gây áp lực lên thị trường chung. Tâm lý thận 
trọng còn thể hiện qua áp lực bán giải chấp ở một số cổ phiếu riêng lẻ như PNJ, SHB..., dù 
một vài nhóm trụ như dầu khí, tiêu dùng có thời điểm đóng vai trò nâng đỡ chỉ số.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 đi ểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 đi ểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao phục 
vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức cao cho 
phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện đáng kể 
trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm phán vẫn 
đang bế tắc và ít có kh ả năng dòng ch ảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối lại ngay 
trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi l ựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đ ầu tư công, chính sách ti ền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

Xem đầy đủ tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành mẫu nến Doji trên khung đồ thị tuần sau hai tuần giảm điểm liên tiếp, 
cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại tại điểm đỡ gần quanh 1710 (đây cũng là chốt chặn cần 
giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn). 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1710 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1670 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

### Page 1

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  科技‧美國

Vertiv 
(VRT.N/ VRT US)

Onecore、CDU與 800VDC為三大推升營運加速成長之動能 
重要訊息 
Vertiv  FY3Q26 獲利優於市場共識 21%，公司上修 FY2026 EPS 財測約 7%
至年增 70%，來自資料中心散熱與電源需求升溫， 收盤股價上漲約 16%。 
評論及分析  
FY3Q26 EPS 年增 78%至 2.21 美元、優於市場共識 22%。Vertiv  FY3Q26 
(2026 年 7-9 月)營收年增 61%、季增 32%至 43.1 億美元，主要受惠資料
中心需求升溫，以及 FY2Q26 因供應端問題遞延之營收約 1-1.5 億美元已於
本季認列。FY3Q26 營利率年增 2.9ppts、季增 2.6ppts 至 25.2%，主要受
惠營利率較佳的美洲地區成長較強勁 ；EPS 年增 78%、季增 45%至 2.21 美
元，優於凱基預估 15%、市場共識預估 22%。 
Vertiv 上修 FY2026 EPS 財測約 7%至 7.15 美元。Vertiv  展望 FY4Q26 營
收年增 53%、季增 2%至 44.1 億美元，若還原 FY3Q26 遞延營收之貢獻，
則兩個季度的營收年增率相當； EPS 財測為年增 65%、季增 2%至 2.25 美
元，大致符合市場共識預估。基於資料中心需求升溫， 公司上調 FY2026 營
收約 5%至 146.5 億美元，隱含年增 43%；EPS 財測上修 7%至 7.15 美
元，隱含年增 70%，優於市場共識預估 7%。 
凱基預估 Vertiv  FY2026/2027 EPS 為 7.32/11.89 美元，年增 62%。
Vertiv 在 2026/10 宣布其 2.3MW CDU 取得 Nvidia 認證，供應鏈評估單台售
價將較 1.3MW 提升 50%，每建置 1GW 資料中心的 CDU 產值約達 2 億美
元，而目前僅三家供應商通過認證 ，凱基預期此將有助於公司提升 CDU 供
貨份額，並據此上修 FY2026/FY2027 EPS 自原預估之 6.91/10.94 美元至
7.32/11.89 美元，年增 62%。 
投資建議  
評等維持「增加持股」，目標價 416 美元 (35x FY2027F EPS) 。 
投資風險 
AI 晶片訂單遞延； CSP 廠放緩資本支出增幅；關稅政策變化影響生產成本。

### Page 2

美國 Vertiv

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Vertiv 營收主要來自資料中心基礎建設解決方案 。Vertiv 業務為提供資料中
心基礎建設解決方案， 營收約 75%來自資料中心客戶。Vertiv 產品組合可分
為硬體 (Products ； FY2Q26 營收占比 80%) 及服務與備品 (Services & 
Spares；20%) 兩大項目。其中  (1) 硬體：提供電源管理、熱管理及整合性
機櫃解決方案，包括交流不斷電供應系統 (UPS)、冷卻系統等；(2) 服務與備
品包含預防性維護、工程和諮詢服務、遠程監控、備件等。FY 2Q26 各地區
營收比重為美洲  68%、亞太地區  19%、歐非中東地區 13%。獲利方面，
美洲基於 AI 資料中心規格要求較高 ，營利率 達 25%-30%、優於公司平
均；歐洲近幾季因關稅成本上揚而使營利率降至 15%-20%；亞太地區因價
格競爭激烈，營利率僅約 10%-15%、低於平均。  
Vertiv 提供完整電源與散熱系統 解決方案，協助客戶建置資料中心 。相較於
散熱或電源廠同業大多僅提供零組件， Vertiv 擁有完整產品線與提供系統建
置的能力；基於下一代 AI 語言模型的運算量與傳輸需求皆顯著提升，使散
熱整合與解決方案的設計難度也隨之向上，讓 Vertiv 相較於僅能提供單一零
組件的廠商更有競爭力。 另外，因應資料中心停機造成損失的問題，Vertiv
也推出 Next Predict ，透過即時遙測與 AI 演算法持續監控 BESS 電池儲能系
統、UPS 與液冷 CDU/泵浦的運作狀態，在異常影響 AI 算力前先行預警，從
而預防損失。Vertiv 資料中心客戶包含 AWS、Meta、Microsoft 、AT&T、
Verizon 、Coreweave 、Oklo，供應模式大多是將電源、散熱與系統等產品
一併提供給客戶，協助其建置資料中心。  
在產品接單上，凱基預期 Vertiv 推出之 OneCore 資料中心建置解決方案將
對整體訂單具有高度貢獻。此解決方案主要是因應伺服器液冷散熱與電源需
求所提供的產品，其採用直接接觸晶片散熱 (Direct -to-Chip Cooling) 技術，
避免晶片在電力故障的幾十秒內因失去冷卻而受損 ，提供建置容量從 10MW
到 1,000MW 的選擇。OneCore 資料中心建置解決方案 之中的 MegaMod ™ 
HDX 主要是針對需要建造一座小型資料中心的客戶所設計，因應數十櫃至數
百櫃的 AI 資料中心建置需求。基於 AI 伺服器機房的液冷散熱管路與電力分
配較為複雜，若由不同供應商安裝可 能會出現漏水或電力供應不足的狀況；
Vertiv 可解決此整合問題，並將安裝時間壓縮在 9-12 個月，相較於大多數
供應商須 18-24 個月而言更為迅速。 目前市場上已有超過 500 家公司有自
建小型資料中心的需求，若以 10MW 方案價格約 8,000 萬美元評估，潛在
市場產值達 400 億美元以上，而觀察目前有提供同等級產品者僅
Schneider( 法)與 Eaton  (美)等同業，使 Vertiv 可望在此領域成為主要供應商
之一， 凱基預期公司 將取得超過 130 億美元訂單，有利於公司
FY2026/FY20 27 營收維持高成長性。  
Vertiv 在 2026/10 宣布其 2.3MW CDU 取得 Nvidia 認證，供應鏈評估單台售
價將較 1.3MW 提升 50%，每建置 1GW 資料中心的 CDU 產值約達 2 億美
元，目前具相同提供能力的供應商僅包含韓國 LG 電子與美國 LiquidStack ，
Vertiv 可望在 CDU 供應鏈提升份額，預期公司可在此市場取得約 20%供貨
份額，並可望貢獻 2027 年營收達 15 億美元。

### Page 3

美國 Vertiv

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  3

圖 1: Vertiv推出 2.3MW CDU，預估貢獻 2027年營收達 15億美元

資料來源：公司資料；凱基

Nvidia 與 ASIC AI 伺服器陸續導入電源櫃，預計 FY2027 顯著貢獻營收。
基於 Nvidia 與 CSP 廠下世代 AI 伺服器功耗皆顯著提升，客戶對於能處理達
200kW 熱能的液冷散熱需求增加，推升散熱產值提升。另外，因各地政府
對 PUE 規範逐步往 1.5 以下的水準靠攏，而現行氣冷散熱之 PUE 普遍落在
1.7 上下的水準，此限制亦使客戶對散熱方案產生升級需求。凱基 預期在 AI
散熱解決方案升級、MegaMod ™ HDX 營收貢獻逐步增加、以及公司可望在
2.3MW CDU 市場提升份額的帶動下，Vertiv FY2026/FY2027 散熱產品營收
可望達 60.9/92.0 億美元，年增 51%。 
電源產品部分，Vertiv  800VDC 產品目前處於驗證階段 ，預計 2027 年開始
貢獻營收，合作對象包含 hyperscaler 及晶片端夥伴。基於其單價約達 50 萬
美元、預估 2027 年出貨量約落在 1,000- 2,000 櫃上下的水準；公司目前亦
有推出 400VDC 電源櫃，初期以 Meta 和 Microsoft 導入較為積極，預期電
源櫃產品可望貢獻 Vertiv  FY2027 營收達 10 億美元，為其 2027 年電源產品
線主要成長動能，預估 Vertiv FY2026/FY2027 電源產品營收為 36.5/60.7 億
美元，年增 66%。

AI伺服器產品

散熱產品
獲利指標

資料來源：Bloomberg；凱基預估 
*財年止於十二月

Vertiv 評等維持「增加持股」，目標價為 416 美元。Vertiv 在資料中心散熱
和電源產品提供的 solution 比大多數同業更完整，營收成長性可望優於產業
平均；凱基預期公司推出的 OneCore 資料中心建置解決方案將拿下超過
130 億美元訂單， 此為支撐公司營運成長之主要動能。另外，公司 在
2026/10 宣布其 2.3MW CDU 取得 Nvidia 認證，供應鏈評估單台售價將較
1.3MW 提升 50%，每建置 1GW 資料中心的 CDU 產值約達 2 億美元，而目
前僅三家供應商通過認證，凱基預期此將有助於公司提升 CDU 供貨份額，
並據此上修 FY2026/FY2027 EPS 自原預估之 6.91/10.94 美元至 7.32/11.89
美元，年增 62%；相較市場共識 EPS 分別為 6.66/9.23 美元，目前股價交易
於約 22/26 倍之 FY2027 凱基與市場共識 EPS 預估。 
Vertiv 過往五年本益比多落於 12-36 倍之間，考量 (1) Vertiv 在電源與散熱
產品之整合能力優於同業表現，並能提供系統級產品，有助公司接單能 力提
升；(2) ASIC 晶片陸續推出且對電源與散熱需求皆提升，可望推升公司未來
兩年獲利成長性優於產業平均；(3) 公司在 2.3MW CDU 與 800VDC 領域皆
為少數供應商之一， FY2027 電源與散熱產品成長性可望進一步提升。綜合
上述原因，預期 Vertiv 本益比將回升至區間上緣之 35 倍上下浮動；基於當
前評價偏低，故維持「增加持股」之 評等，目標價則基於預估 EPS 上修而調
升至 416 美元 (35x FY2027F EPS )。

## Document 373: European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

European resilience, American vigour

Eurozone growth continues to prove resilient, notwithstanding the volatility of the Irish figures. The Q3 data reinforce 
our forecast of 1.1% growth this year, which could even be slightly exceeded, as German and Spanish momentum shows 
no sign of fading. In France, the negative surprise (-0.1% q/q against a consensus of +0.1% q/q) stems from a new one-
off factor (the sharp fall in aerospace output caused by delays in input deliveries) and masks an encouraging rebound 
in household consumption. In the United  States, growth reached its highest level since Q2 2025 (0.8% q/q), driven by 
buoyant private domestic demand.

Eurozone: The resilience of growth is confirmed 
Eurozone GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3  according to Eurostat's preliminary flash estimate, above both the consensus and our 
Nowcast (0.3%). The ever -volatile Irish figures contributed 0.2 pp to this outcome. Excluding Ireland, growth (0.2% q/q) 
nonetheless remains in line with the pace of recent quarters (0.2 -0.3% q/q on average). This illustrates the Eurozone's capacity 
to withstand the current shocks, thanks in particular to the strength of Spain (+0.07 pp contribution to the q/q change in Eurozone’s 
GDP) and the Netherlands (+0.04 pp), and to Germany's gradual recovery (+0.06 pp). France (-0.03 pp) and Italy (zero growth), 
by contrast, posted disappointing results, although in France's case these partly reflect temporary factors (aerospace output). The 
growth carry-over for 2026 now stands at 1.0%, which supports our forecast of 1.1% in annual average terms and even points to 
upside risk. The European Commission's October surveys remain upbeat, with a further improvement in industrial activity 
expectations. 
The German recovery continues . As anticipated by our  Nowcast, real GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3 (after 0.3% in Q2).  
According to Destatis, consumption (private and public) increased slightly, while investment edged down. Export momentum 
slowed markedly compared with Q2. This is Germany's fourth consecutive quarter of growth. 
Italian GDP surprised on the downside, stagnating in Q3 (against a consensus of 0.1%).  According to Istat, this reflects a 
decline in value added in industry, offset by increases in services and agriculture. The positive contribution from domestic demand 
was reportedly cancelled out by a negative contribution from net trade. 
Spanish growth remained robust in Q3, in line with expectations (0.6% q/q, after 0.7% in Q2),  driven mainly by domestic 
demand (0.5 pp contribution) and, to a lesser extent, by net exports (0.1 pp). Consumption remains the main engine of growth 
(+0.6% q/q for private consumption and +0.9% for public consumption). Investment rose by 0.7%. Export gr owth (1.3%) once 
again outpaced import growth (1.1%) this quarter. All sectors except construction recorded positive changes in value added, with 
industry and services both up 0.7%. 
France: A sharp drop in aerospace output prevents a return to growth in Q3 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 (after stagnating in Q2), weighed down by a sharp fall in aerospace output (-4.5% q/q 
in the production of other transport equipment, which includes aerospace). This steep decline dragged down goods production 
(down 1.2% q/q) and explains the sharp contraction in exports ( -1.7% q/q) and business investment (-0.4% q/q). Net trade thus 
subtracted 0.6 pp from GDP (imports were flat over the same period). Conversely, household consumption surprised on the 
upside (+0.4% q/q), boosted by purchases of electric vehicles (registrations up 112% y/y in Q3). Public consumption 
(+0.3% q/q) also supported growth, as did activity in market services (+0.3% q/q).  Despite the sharp drop in aerospace 
output in Q3, Airbus has confirmed its delivery targets for the year (with engine supply problems expected to be resolved), pointing 
to a strong rebound in growth in Q4 (0.3% q/q according to our forecasts). Overall, gr owth is expected to reach 0.3% in 2026 , 
before picking up to 0.8% in 2027. 
United States: Growth at a one-year high  
GDP growth picked up in Q3 to 3.4% at a seasonally adjusted annualised rate ( AR; i.e. 0.8% q/q), from 2.2% in Q2 
(0.6% q/q). This is the fastest pace since Q3 2025, confirming that activity is almost immune to the energy shock. Private domestic 
demand remains particularly strong. Private non-residential investment accelerated to 12.7% AR, a performance unmatched since 
the post-Covid reopening. AI-related components are driving this figure (18.5% AR), but other types of investment are also rising 
for a second consecutive quarter (3.8% AR) after contracting uninterruptedly throughout 2025. Household consumption, steady 
at 2.5% AR in real terms, is growing faster than it did before inflation picked up again, but at the cost of a significant drop in the 
saving rate (3.8% at the end of the quarter, down from 4.4% at the end of the previous quarter). The United States nonetheles s 
continues to "import its growth": net trade is weighing on GDP owing to a surge in imports (19.7% AR), driven by AI -related 
components – IT, semiconductors and telecommunications (103.8% AR).

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of Advanced Economies Team, France  
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

態，體現實質 政策利率低於 緊縮利率 區間與
彭博金 融情勢指數變化不大 ，當前美國貨幣
政策與市場流動性仍偏寬鬆 ，倘若地緣擾動

持續造成通 膨居高難下 ，恐進一步推升聯準
會升息預期 ，疊加政府財政 風險與貨幣政策
透明度降 ，加劇長 天期公債利率 上行風險 ，
長天期債以交易為主，不建議長期持有。

► 特斯拉3Q26財報好壞參半 — 毛利率與交付
量超預 期 ， 營收 大 致符合 ，營業 利潤 遜預
期，最大意外是自由現金流達 15億美元，高
於共識負43億，資本開支指引下調成憂慮。
► 早前公司展示下代 Roadster能力，本質上是
光環車 ，定價高受眾少 ，2028 年才有望投
產，盈利貢獻有限 。Model 3/Y成為大眾商
品，競爭激烈 ，利潤率壓縮 。正如傳統品牌
以賽道或參賽展示技術 ，「飛天」旨在展示
獨有跨界整合能力，維持品牌形象溢價。
► 至於和SpaceX的潛在合併，核心在於貫通多
頂 業 務， 提 高 資 本 運 用 效 率， 共同分擔
Terafab 的開支等 ，現時兩家合計市值約為
3.5兆美元。這項巨型潛在交易 ，除了要跨過
極高表決門檻外，SpaceX具極敏感的美國國
防及NASA合作背景 ，而特斯拉有超過 20%
業務倚重上海超級工廠 ，合併存在嚴重地緣
衝突。預期馬斯克將在川普離任前 ，加速執
行Tesla與SpaceX 合併。

► 今年9月適逢地緣擾動持續推升高油價、聯準
會重啟 升息 、財政部 與企 業發債 浪潮 等 影
響，形塑債市今年以來單月最差表現 。檢視
2021年以來美國投資級債前十大單月最差總
報酬，2022年聯準會 3月啟動升息年度就佔
一半以上，顯示利率風險仍為近5年來債市最
大風險 ，然檢視單月最差報酬表現未來期間
總報酬，除2022年1月和3月聯準會重啟升息
前夕與當月表現明顯較弱，多數於未來1年後
多呈現正報酬 ，反映隨利率走升放大孳息效
益，同時挹注債券長期持有之報酬空間。
► 今年來債券市場利率波動明顯推升信用債利
差，其 中僅 以低 信 評債利 差升幅 較顯 ，此
外，9月以來利率連動度高及AI相關產業債種
也成為利差擴大重災區 ，凸顯債市投資人減
輕市場不確定性的投資傾向 ，考量信用債市
歷經利率上行利空洗禮 ，信用利差同步擴大

創造收益空間 ，可把握債信體質仍佳企業債
鎖利良機，並透過時間複利力量強化收益。
利率波動升，僅低信評利差擴大程度相對明顯9月為美國投資級債今年最差單月報酬
用時間換空間，單月最差表現形塑未來報酬機會

### Page 8

8
► 受聯準會重啟升息影響 ，過去一年美國公債
殖利率曲線持續陡峭化 ，其中利率最大升幅
落在短天期的 2至3年期間，美國核心通膨與
就業仍具韌性 ，聯準會將持續保持 緊縮貨幣
政策步調來應對高通膨 ，考量美國高物價與
高利率環境短期 難消，宜透過縮短債券配置
天期並進行動態調整 ，為因應後續利率不確
定性環境的良策。
► 為因應利率不確定性 ，投資人可採取債券梯
的投資配置 ，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行 組合，債券梯投資重點主要在
於，根據殖利率曲線型態及利率動態建立不
同期間 債券梯，假設市場利率走升且殖利率
曲線正斜率 ，建立短天期債券梯除了降低利
率風險 ，有 短期 閒 置資金 及孳息 需求 投資
人，亦可取得穩定孳息 收益。若後續投資人
有短期資金需求 ，可以視情況將短期債券到
期後資金回收 ，降低只投資單一債券須持至
到期或直接變現的流動性風險。
投資焦點
美國公債殖利率曲線持續為正斜率
高利率波動持續，用策略迎戰市場擾動
資料來源：Bloomberg，凱基證券整理

建立短天期債券梯，分散風險及穩定現金流
債券梯
經過第2年第一檔 債券到期，視資金需求 及市況與利率 動
態決定後續配置

短天期債券梯 ：假設買入 2檔各 3、4、5及7年到期債券 ，
聚焦 短期穩定收益
► 債券梯是一種分散投資方式 ，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行組合，可兼具：
1) 分散利率風險： 將資金分別買進不同到期年期的債券 ，以分
散利率風險。
2) 分散信用風險： 分散投資不同發行 機構債券，不會重壓 單一
債券並可分散信用違約風險。
3) 降低流動性風險： 若投資人有短期資金需求 ，可以視情況將
短期債券到期後資金回收 ，降低只投資單一債券須持至到期
或直接變現之流動性風險。

### Page 9

9
股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境 使中短天期債券提供
吸引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後
利差具吸引力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等

匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高 ，使金價短期承壓 ，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化 ，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。

### Page 18

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 419: New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-09)

Source PDF: all report pdf/New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-09).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• As polls suggested, the election was a nailbiter
that has not yet been resolved. Election night
results show a lead to the centre-right on
paper in a significantly enlarged parliament.
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First
and ACT won 67 seats. The centre-left bloc
of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti Māori won 57
seats. There was a swing away from National
to its coalition partners while the centre left
grouping picked up a few seats.

• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling
and will enter parliament with at least 7 seats,
reducing what could have been a larger swing
to the centre-left grouping.
• The size of parliament will expand by around 9
seats as National held onto a large share of its
electorate votes, entitling it to more seats than
implied by its party vote share. As a result,
a bloc would require 66 seats to command a
majority in parliament.
• Several senior National ministers have not

been re-elected, including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. She will continue in a caretaker
role but will need to be replaced in any new
centre-right government.
• Significant uncertainty about the result exists.
National might lose perhaps two marginal
electorates in the final analysis. There are
400,000 special votes to count which may
shift the list seat allocations as these tend
to reduce the share of the centre-right bloc
by 1.6-1.7ppts. Hence, when all is said and

done, the centre-right’s 10 seat advantage on
election night could reduce by 1-4 seats.
• Should this play out the centre-right would not
have sufficient seats to reach a majority in an
enlarged house and would require the support
of the Opportunity Party to govern.
• The final election outcome will not be
known until 27 November. Serious coalition
negotiations likely won’t begin until then
and delays could occur. Should recounts be
required, some further delay is possible.

• Based on these results, we see a higher
probability of a centre-right government
ultimately emerging that likely will require
the support of the Opportunity Party in some
form. The Opportunity Party likely won’t enter
Cabinet but may offer support on confidence
and supply in exchange for policy concessions.
• But a centre-left government can’t be ruled
out if special votes heavily favour the centre
left bloc, and especially if an agreement
between the Opportunity Party and the centre-

right parties can’t be reached.
• Regardless of the final count, the results
suggest a government with weak support and
ongoing vulnerabilities. Hence the prospect of
controversial large changes may be modest.
For a centre-right government that might
limit the extent of fiscal consolidation, while
the centre left’s plans for larger scale tax and
spending initiatives could be trimmed back.
• A centre-right government won’t change the
RBNZ mandate while a centre-left government

likely still will. A new finance minister will be
required with the possibility of ACT leader
David Seymour taking on that role.
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
New Zealand 2026 General Election:
Preliminary results and next steps.
9 Nov 2026 |  Westpac Economics Team | westpac.co.nz/economics | economics@westpac.co.nz
Kelly Eckhold, Chief Economist | +64 9 348 9382 | +64 21 786 758 | kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
The result.
The preliminary results of the 2026 General Election,
based on the election night count, are set out in the table
above. In summary:
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First and ACT
won 67 seats.
• The centre-left bloc of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti
Māori won 57 seats.
• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling and will
enter parliament with 7 seats.
These results indicate a swing away from National to its

coalition partners while the centre-left grouping picked
up a few seats. The big gainer was the Opportunity Party
who perhaps took a big share of what otherwise could
have been a larger swing to the centre-left grouping.
The size of parliament will expand by around nine seats,
as National held onto a large share of its electorate
votes and hence has more seats than it would have been
entitled to based on its party vote share. As a result, a
governing bloc would require 66 seats to command a

majority in parliament.
Several of the electorate seats are very tight (see Table
2), so election night majorities could easily be overturned
once special votes are counted. Of particular relevance
is the seat of Wairarapa, where the National candidate is
leading the Labour candidate by just 139 votes. If Labour
were to win that seat (specials favoured Labour in 2020
but National in 2023), all else equal the centre-right seat
count would decline to 66 seats in what would become

a 130-seat Parliament – just enough to command a
majority. But if either NZ First or ACT were to lose a single
party vote seat to the left bloc once specials are counted
then that majority would disappear.
On that score, historically the election night results have
tended to overstate the support of the centre-right bloc
and understate the support for parties on the left of
the political spectrum. For example, over the previous
three elections (2017, 2020, 2023), the current centre-

right bloc’s vote share has declined 1.9%, 1.7% and 1.6%
respectively following the counting of special votes, with
the National Party losing 2 seats on each occasion once
the official results were declared (Labour, the Greens and
Te Pāti Māori have benefited). On this occasion, given its
overhang, a lower party vote share would not lower the
National Party’s final vote count, although it could alter
the size of its overhang and thus the number of seats

required to command a majority. In recent elections both
NZ First and ACT have seen their vote shares decline
following the counting of special votes.
The process from here.
Official results.
The official results will be declared on 27 November,
adding the results of an estimated 400,000 special votes
(with around a quarter of these cast overseas) to the
2.41m votes counted on election night.
We expect discussions between potential coalition
partners will begin immediately to start sketching out the

parameters of a coalition agreement. But given the tight
margins of the current vote count, no firm positions will
be agreed until the results are declared.
The writ and recounts.
After 27 November, the writ is scheduled to be returned
on 3 December – formally certifying who has been
elected. However, that date is subject to any recounts

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
that might be requested. Parliament is required to meet
no later than 14 January (six weeks after the return of the
writ), providing an opportunity for a new government
to test that it has the confidence of the House. It is
worth noting that Parliament can be summoned and
begin functioning even if coalition negotiations are still
occurring. If no coalition or confidence-and-supply
arrangement has been reached by then, negotiations can

simply continue and the incumbent Government remains
in office under the caretaker convention (Ministers in
the outgoing government retain executive authority
temporarily, even if they are no longer MPs).
Given that the Pre-Election Economic and Fiscal
Update was released in late September there is no legal
requirement for the Treasury to publish a Half Year
Economic and Fiscal Update (HYEFU) this year. And given
that it will be some weeks before a government is formed,

it seems unlikely that there will be a government in place
that would request that a HYEFU be produced despite
there being no statutory requirement (certainly there
would be little time to incorporate policy changes that
fall out of new coalition agreements, thus diminishing
its value). Once a government is formed, it will have an
opportunity to lay out its immediate priorities in the
Budget Policy Statement (BPS), which by law needs to
be released before the end of March next year. The new

government could decide to release an interim fiscal
update with the BPS if that was thought necessary.
Potential coalition outcomes.
We see three main likely outcomes:
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First.
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First with the support of the Opportunity Party
on confidence and supply.
• Some coalition of Labour, the Greens, Opportunity and
Te Pāti Māori, potentially with Opportunity and Te Pāti

Māori sitting outside the cabinet.
Our preview of the election discussed the policies of
the main political parties, while the figure on page 5
highlights some points of commonality with the centre-
right and centre-left blocs.
National, ACT and NZ First share a broadly pro-
development and deregulatory agenda, including support
for greater resource development, fewer regulatory
barriers and compulsory or expanded KiwiSaver. Beyond
that common core, National and ACT align particularly

closely on lower taxes and deregulation, while National
and NZ First share an emphasis on infrastructure, regional
development and law and order. ACT and NZ First favour
tighter immigration settings and tighter welfare policies.
The differences are clearest at the edges: ACT advocates
deeper tax cuts, smaller government and more market-
led provision, while NZ First places greater emphasis
on economic sovereignty, regional development and
targeted support for working New Zealanders. National

occupies more of the middle ground, combining fiscal
discipline and a pro-business agenda with increased
infrastructure and housing supply.
The Opportunity Party presents itself as a middle
alternative that can work with the left or the right
groupings. They have five bottom line policies they say
they need to support any new government. These include
competition reform through enhanced Commerce
Commission powers, more restrictions on political
lobbyists and donors, a ten-year plan of investment in the

healthcare sector, an end to bottom trawling in inshore
areas and a phase out elsewhere and for the Government
to recommit to New Zealand’s Paris Agreement
obligations. Their success in meeting these bottom
lines with a new government and their flexibility around
these requirements will be key to their ability to form an
agreement with either side. There is better congruence
of the Opportunity Party’s policies with the left than the
right – so progress here will be critical to watch should

their support be required to form a new government. We
suspect they will be very motivated to avoid the need for
a new election via a hung parliament should push come
to shove.

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Labour, the Greens and Opportunity share support
for increased public and social investment, a broader
tax base, greater infrastructure investment, stronger
competition policy, cheaper public transport and a
more active role for government. Labour and the Greens
overlap particularly on higher taxation, public investment
and stronger climate and environmental policy, while
Labour and Opportunity share an emphasis on social

investment and higher public-service wages. The Greens
and Opportunity both favour broader taxation and
stronger climate action, although their preferred tax
instruments differ. Their distinctive priorities are also
clear: Labour places more weight on worker protections
and restoring the RBNZ’s dual mandate; the Greens on
environmental protection, progressive taxation and Te
Tiriti; and Opportunity on land-value taxation, housing
infrastructure, productivity, innovation and evidence-

based policy.

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Turning point?
• We expect the unemployment rate to edge
down to 5.5% for the September quarter.
• Job numbers have been gaining momentum
in recent months, and now look to be enough
to outstrip population growth. However, the
participation rate will be the deciding factor.
• Despite cost-of-living pressures on workers,
the existing slack in the labour market is likely
to keep wage growth subdued.
• Our forecasts are a little more positive than

what the RBNZ assumed in their September
policy statement. Assuming they deliver on
an OCR hike this afternoon, the labour market
surveys could have an important bearing on
whether they follow up with another hike in
December.

The New Zealand economy has returned to growth over
the last year, despite a number of headwinds. That’s been
accompanied by some modest growth in jobs, though
to date it hasn’t been enough to prevent a further rise

in the unemployment rate. However, we think that next
Wednesday’s labour market reports could show the jobs
market reaching a turning point.
We expect the unemployment rate to ease from 5.6%
to 5.5% in the September quarter, which would mark
the first fall in almost five years. That’s not a firmly held
view, given the usual margin of uncertainty around this
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
Preview of Q3 labour market surveys:
4 November, 10:45am.
28 Oct 2026 | Michael Gordon, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5670 | michael.gordon@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
measure. But we’re now seeing consistent evidence
that jobs growth is outstripping the modest pace of
population growth. We also note that the previous result
was 5.56% in unrounded terms, so it wouldn’t take much
to tip the rounded rate down to 5.5% this time.
On the whole, our forecasts are a little stronger than
what the RBNZ assumed in its September Monetary
Policy Statement. While our unemployment rate forecast

is identical, we’re more positive on both employment
and labour force participation. Meanwhile, our pick of a
0.5% rise in the Labour Cost Index is slightly ahead of the
RBNZ’s estimate of 0.4%.
Assuming that the RBNZ delivers the OCR hike that we
expect this afternoon, we think they will be noncommittal
about the chances of another hike at the 9 December
Monetary Policy Statement. In that respect, the labour
market surveys could be important in setting market

expectations for the December review, as there aren’t
that many major data releases between now and then.
Forecast details.
We expect the Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS)
to show a 0.5% rise in employment for the September
quarter. That’s broadly in line with the Monthly
Employment Indicator (MEI), with the September release
this morning showing a third straight month of growth
in filled jobs. We note that employment growth in the
HLFS has actually outstripped the MEI for the last nine

quarters, but it’s not clear why this has been the case and
we’re wary of extrapolating it too far into the next quarter.

Employment growth of around 0.5% would be more than
enough to absorb the modest 0.3% rise in the working-
age population, and on its own would suggest a fall in

the unemployment rate. But it also comes down to the
rate of labour force participation, which we don’t really
have much guidance on. The participation rate has been
choppy over recent quarters, and in the June quarter
we saw an unusually large 0.3pt rise to 70.7%; we’re
assuming some payback this time with a more modest
0.1pt rise to 70.8%.
We’ll also be keeping an eye on the broader indicators of
slack in the labour market, although we don’t formally use

these in our forecasts. The June quarter saw a sharp rise
in the underutilisation rate from 12.9% to 13.8%. However,
we think there’s some residual seasonality in this series,
relating to one component in particular: people who are
available for work but not actively looking. In that case,
we could see a substantial pullback in the underutilisation
measure this time.
Previously we’ve characterised the labour market as a
“low-fire, low-hire” environment, with much of the rise in

unemployment reflecting the difficulty of breaking into
the workforce, rather than job losses. There are some
signs of a thaw emerging, with job vacancies picking up
from their lows over the last year and business surveys
pointing to a lift in hiring intentions. But we still expect
that it will be a long road to recovery – our current
forecasts see the unemployment rate at 5% by the end of
next year.
While cost-of-living pressures are again rearing their

head as a major concern for workers, the existing degree
of slack in the labour market will continue to make it
difficult for them to push for bigger pay increases. We
expect a 0.5% rise in the Labour Cost Index (LCI) for
the September quarter, which would nudge the annual
growth rate up slightly to 2.1%. That said, we don’t have a
lot to go on for this forecast. The MEI includes a measure
of median weekly earnings, which has shown no sign of
acceleration so far – but this is only a very loose indicator

of wage pressures, as it will also reflect any changes in
the composition of jobs and pay rates.

### Page 3

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
US-China talks, a possible Trump-Xi-Putin trilateral meeting, and any signs of a China-Japan diplomatic
thaw will be critical watchpoints. The priorities of the summit between representatives of 21 Asia-Pacific
economies are set to include efforts to boost innovation, digital connectivity, supply-chain resilience and

support for an open multilateral trading system. During the summit, presidents Trump and Xi are expected to
meet for the third time this year. This meeting will provide signals on the state of China-US talks, including the
operationalisation of new bilateral trade and investment boards, and on the likelihood of another extension of
the Busan trade truce beyond January 2027. President Trump has also indicated he is open to a trilateral US-
China-Russia meeting during the summit, possibly covering nuclear arms control and the Russia-Ukraine war.

Investors will also closely watch whether Japanese Prime Minister Takaichi and President Xi meet on the
sidelines, following reports that Tokyo is hoping to arrange a meeting. However, the bar for a formal
breakthrough will likely remain high. As we noted in a report earlier this year, a full normalisation of relations
and an easing of Chinese economic restrictions – which include rare-earth export controls – may require a
lengthy period of diplomatic re-engagement and backchannel diplomacy.

A divided government could mean less change at home but more abroad. Third-party polling and betting
markets forecast Democrat control of the House, and an increasing probability of flipping the Senate. 80% of
respondents in our monthly poll now say living costs will influence their vote – the most since April. For
markets, a divided government could mean less domestic legislative change, but not necessarily a more
predictable US foreign policy. Losing Congress could leave theadministration’s geopolitical and geoeconomic

toolkit largely intact.
In our view, weapons stockpiles, inflationary pressures and long-end yields may matter more than Congress
in constraining theadministration’s choices in 2027 and 2028. A Democratic House could dial up oversight of
the administration, launch investigations, force votes on War Powers Resolutions, and use appropriations bills
to press for restrictions on departmental funding. This may generate a steady stream of attention-grabbing

headlines, but whether it decisively affects the event-driven risk that matters for markets (e.g. blocking a tariff
or preventing a limited military strike) remains in doubt. If domestic policymaking becomes more challenging,
foreign policy could become a top priority for the Trump administration.
In the spotlight for November 2026: US midterms and APEC Summit
US midterm elections (3 November)
APEC Economic Leaders’ Meeting in Shenzhen (18-19 November)
